CategoryTheory.ShortComplex.colimitCoconedefMathlib.Algebra.Homology.ShortComplex.Limits, function or constant
Wording
the colimit cocone of F (from the name)
English
Let $F$ be a functor of $J$ and the set of short complexes of $C$. Then the colimit cocone of $F$ is a cocone of $F$.

English

Mathlib.Algebra.IsPrimePow

IsPrimePowdefMathlib.Algebra.IsPrimePow, predicate
Wording
n is a prime power (by hand)
English
Let $n$ be an element of $R$. Then $n$ is a prime power, if there exists an element $p$ of $R$, such that there exists a natural number $k$, such that $p$ is prime, $0 < k$ and $\EqtMACRoH {p ^ {k}}{n}$.
